- **cytosine-methylation-frequency-comparison**: Use when you have DeepSignal-plant call_freq methylation-frequency tables (chrom, pos, strand, coverage, modification_frequency) for two Nanopore samples and pre-computed repeat pairs (length ≥100, identity ≥0.99) and need to quantify differentially methylated repeat pairs. - **cytosine-site-screening**: Use when you have a genome reference (e.g. GCF_000001735.4_TAIR10.1_genomic.fna for A. thaliana), Nanopore-derived per-site methylation frequencies, and need to identify repeat pairs and differentially methylated cytosines between duplicated regions.
